# SBXR module and function catalog — 2026-09-12

The V4 producer entries below are historical. Their source links resolve to
commit `0859e96`, where the retired producer remains available for interpreting
this dated catalog; they do not describe the current MVP path.

Companion to [the MVP qualification review](2026-09-12-mvp-qualification-review.md). This is a navigation inventory of the current working tree, including existing uncommitted source files. It creates no contract, baseline or validation requirement. Go declarations were enumerated with the standard Go parser; counts include named methods and platform alternatives, and exclude test helpers and anonymous closures. Scenario mappings describe responsibility/coverage themes, not a measured runtime call graph. A mapping does not prove that a test exercised every function.

Function purpose is explained at the owning file/module level below; exact declaration names link to source. Exported Go symbols include private collaboration and adapter methods, not just owner-facing operations. Types are listed to expose the smaller modules and internal seams without treating every struct as an independent product module. Embedded installer shell/template code remains part of its enclosing Go file.

## cmd/sbxr-release

### cmd/sbxr-release/bootstrap.go

Generated bootstrap installer and exact-release removal restoration shell program.

**Qualification relevance:** 01, 06, 19, 25; restoration variants are automated-only.

**All named functions / methods:** [buildBootstrapFile](/Users/Albert/Documents/Codex/SBXR/cmd/sbxr-release/bootstrap.go:15).

### cmd/sbxr-release/gateway.go

Qualification-only HTTP transport for candidate assets and evidence exchange.

**Qualification relevance:** Harness for 01–25; no installed proxy feature.

**Named types / smaller seams:** [gatewayManifest](/Users/Albert/Documents/Codex/SBXR/cmd/sbxr-release/gateway.go:24), [gatewayAsset](/Users/Albert/Documents/Codex/SBXR/cmd/sbxr-release/gateway.go:45), [gatewayRelease](/Users/Albert/Documents/Codex/SBXR/cmd/sbxr-release/gateway.go:52), [qualificationGateway](/Users/Albert/Documents/Codex/SBXR/cmd/sbxr-release/gateway.go:58).

## internal/proxyinstallation/subscriptionserving

### internal/proxyinstallation/subscriptionserving/serving.go

## Qualification/workflow program inventory

The following lists all 140 `.py`, `.sh` and workflow `.yml` files below `.github` in this working tree, including harness tests. Python classes, nested named functions and methods are AST-enumerated. Shell named functions and embedded Python `def` statements are line-enumerated. Top-level script operations have no function name and are represented by the script entry itself. Generated strings/anonymous functions are not separate declarations. Broad family mappings are navigational, not claims of exact runtime coverage.
